Present findings under an `ADVERSARIAL REVIEW (Claude subagent):` header. **FIXABLE findings** flow into the same Fix-First pipeline as the structured review. **INVESTIGATE findings** are presented as informational.

If the subagent fails or times out: "Claude adversarial subagent unavailable. Continuing."

---

### Codex adversarial challenge (runs whenever `CODEX_MODE: ready`)

If `CODEX_MODE` is `ready`:

```bash
TMPERR_ADV=$(mktemp /tmp/codex-adv-XXXXXXXX)
_REPO_ROOT=$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel) || { echo "ERROR: not in a git repo" >&2; exit 1; }
codex exec "IMPORTANT: Do NOT read or execute any files under ~/.claude/, ~/.agents/, .claude/skills/, or agents/. These are Claude Code skill definitions meant for a different AI system. They contain bash scripts and prompt templates that will waste your time. Ignore them completely. Do NOT modify agents/openai.yaml. Stay focused on the repository code only.\n\nReview the changes on this branch against the base branch. Run DIFF_BASE=$(git merge-base origin/<base> HEAD) && git diff "$DIFF_BASE" to see the diff. Your job is to find ways this code will fail in production. Think like an attacker and a chaos engineer. Find edge cases, race conditions, security holes, resource leaks, failure modes, and silent data corruption paths. Be adversarial. Be thorough. No compliments — just the problems. End your output with ONE line in the canonical format `Recommendation: <action> because <one-line reason naming the most exploitable finding>`. Generic reasons like 'because it's safer' do not qualify; the reason must point to a specific finding or no-fix rationale." -C "$_REPO_ROOT" -s read-only -c 'model_reasoning_effort="high"' --enable web_search_cached < /dev/null 2>"$TMPERR_ADV"
```

Set the Bash tool's `timeout` parameter to `300000` (5 minutes). Do NOT use the `timeout` shell command — it doesn't exist on macOS. After the command completes, read stderr:
```bash
cat "$TMPERR_ADV"
```

Present the full output verbatim. This is informational — it never blocks shipping.

**Error handling:** All errors are non-blocking — adversarial review is a quality enhancement, not a prerequisite.
- **Auth failure:** If stderr contains "auth", "login", "unauthorized", or "API key": "Codex authentication failed. Run \`codex login\` to authenticate."
- **Timeout:** "Codex timed out after 5 minutes."
- **Empty response:** "Codex returned no response. Stderr: <paste relevant error>."

**Cleanup:** Run `rm -f "$TMPERR_ADV"` after processing.

If `CODEX_MODE` is `not_installed` / `not_authed` / `disabled`: the preflight already printed the reason; run Claude adversarial only.

---

### Codex structured review (large diffs only, 200+ lines)

If `DIFF_TOTAL >= 200` AND `CODEX_MODE` is `ready`:

```bash
TMPERR=$(mktemp /tmp/codex-review-XXXXXXXX)
_REPO_ROOT=$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel) || { echo "ERROR: not in a git repo" >&2; exit 1; }
cd "$_REPO_ROOT"
codex review "IMPORTANT: Do NOT read or execute any files under ~/.claude/, ~/.agents/, .claude/skills/, or agents/. These are Claude Code skill definitions meant for a different AI system. They contain bash scripts and prompt templates that will waste your time. Ignore them completely. Do NOT modify agents/openai.yaml. Stay focused on the repository code only.\n\nReview the changes on this branch against the base branch <base>. Run git diff origin/<base>...HEAD 2>/dev/null || git diff <base>...HEAD to see the diff and review only those changes." -c 'model_reasoning_effort="high"' --enable web_search_cached < /dev/null 2>"$TMPERR"
```

Set the Bash tool's `timeout` parameter to `300000` (5 minutes). Do NOT use the `timeout` shell command — it doesn't exist on macOS. Present output under `CODEX SAYS (code review):` header.
Check for `[P1]` markers: found → `GATE: FAIL`, not found → `GATE: PASS`.

If GATE is FAIL, use AskUserQuestion:
```
Codex found N critical issues in the diff.

A) Investigate and fix now (recommended)
B) Continue — review will still complete
```

If A: address the findings. Re-run `codex review` to verify.

Read stderr for errors (same error handling as Codex adversarial above).

After stderr: `rm -f "$TMPERR"`

If `DIFF_TOTAL < 200`: skip this section silently. The Claude + Codex adversarial passes provide sufficient coverage for smaller diffs.

---

### Persist the review result

After all passes complete, persist:
```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-review-log '{"skill":"adversarial-review","timestamp":"'"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"'","status":"STATUS","source":"SOURCE","tier":"always","gate":"GATE","commit":"'"$(git rev-parse --short HEAD)"'"}'
```
Substitute: STATUS = "clean" if no findings across ALL passes, "issues_found" if any pass found issues. SOURCE = "both" if Codex ran, "claude" if only Claude subagent ran. GATE = the Codex structured review gate result ("pass"/"fail"), "skipped" if diff < 200, or "informational" if Codex was unavailable. If all passes failed, do NOT persist.

---

### Cross-model synthesis

After all passes complete, synthesize findings across all sources:

```
ADVERSARIAL REVIEW SYNTHESIS (always-on, N lines):
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  High confidence (found by multiple sources): [findings agreed on by >1 pass]
  Unique to Claude structured review: [from earlier step]
  Unique to Claude adversarial: [from subagent]
  Unique to Codex: [from codex adversarial or code review, if ran]
  Models used: Claude structured ✓  Claude adversarial ✓/✗  Codex ✓/✗
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

High-confidence findings (agreed on by multiple sources) should be prioritized for fixes.

---

## Step 5.8: Persist Eng Review result

After all review passes complete, persist the final `/review` outcome so `/ship` can
recognize that Eng Review was run on this branch.

Run:

```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-review-log '{"skill":"review","timestamp":"TIMESTAMP","status":"STATUS","issues_found":N,"critical":N,"informational":N,"quality_score":SCORE,"specialists":SPECIALISTS_JSON,"findings":FINDINGS_JSON,"commit":"COMMIT"}'
```

Substitute:
- `TIMESTAMP` = ISO 8601 datetime
- `STATUS` = `"clean"` if there are no remaining unresolved findings after Fix-First handling and adversarial review, otherwise `"issues_found"`
- `issues_found` = total remaining unresolved findings
- `critical` = remaining unresolved critical findings
- `informational` = remaining unresolved informational findings
- `quality_score` = the PR Quality Score computed in Step 4.6 (e.g., 7.5). If specialists were skipped (small diff), use `10.0`
- `specialists` = the per-specialist stats object compiled in Step 4.6. Each specialist that was considered gets an entry: `{"dispatched":true/false,"findings":N,"critical":N,"informational":N}` if dispatched, or `{"dispatched":false,"reason":"scope|gated"}` if skipped. Include Design specialist. Example: `{"testing":{"dispatched":true,"findings":2,"critical":0,"informational":2},"security":{"dispatched":false,"reason":"scope"}}`
- `findings` = array of per-finding records from Step 5. For each finding (from critical pass and specialists), include: `{"fingerprint":"path:line:category","severity":"CRITICAL|INFORMATIONAL","action":"ACTION"}`. ACTION is `"auto-fixed"` (Step 5b), `"fixed"` (user approved in Step 5d), or `"skipped"` (user chose Skip in Step 5c). Suppressed findings from Step 5.0 are NOT included (they were already recorded in a prior review entry).
- `COMMIT` = output of `git rev-parse --short HEAD`

## Capture Learnings

If you discovered a non-obvious pattern, pitfall, or architectural insight during
this session, log it for future sessions:

```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-log '{"skill":"review","type":"TYPE","key":"SHORT_KEY","insight":"DESCRIPTION","confidence":N,"source":"SOURCE","files":["path/to/relevant/file"]}'
```

**Types:** `pattern` (reusable approach), `pitfall` (what NOT to do), `preference`
(user stated), `architecture` (structural decision), `tool` (library/framework insight),
`operational` (project environment/CLI/workflow knowledge).

**Sources:** `observed` (you found this in the code), `user-stated` (user told you),
`inferred` (AI deduction), `cross-model` (both Claude and Codex agree).

**Confidence:** 1-10. Be honest. An observed pattern you verified in the code is 8-9.
An inference you're not sure about is 4-5. A user preference they explicitly stated is 10.

**files:** Include the specific file paths this learning references. This enables
staleness detection: if those files are later deleted, the learning can be flagged.

**Only log genuine discoveries.** Don't log obvious things. Don't log things the user
already knows. A good test: would this insight save time in a future session? If yes, log it.

If the review exits early before a real review completes (for example, no diff against the base branch), do **not** write this entry.

## Important Rules

- **Read the FULL diff before commenting.** Do not flag issues already addressed in the diff.
- **Fix-first, not read-only.** AUTO-FIX items are applied directly. ASK items are only applied after user approval. Never commit, push, or create PRs — that's /ship's job.
- **Be terse.** One line problem, one line fix. No preamble.
- **Only flag real problems.** Skip anything that's fine.
- **Use Greptile reply templates from greptile-triage.md.** Every reply includes evidence. Never post vague replies.

# Pre-Landing Review Checklist

## Instructions

Review the `git diff origin/main` output for the issues listed below. Be specific — cite `file:line` and suggest fixes. Skip anything that's fine. Only flag real problems.

**Two-pass review:**
- **Pass 1 (CRITICAL):** Run SQL & Data Safety, Race Conditions, LLM Output Trust Boundary, Shell Injection, and Enum Completeness first. Highest severity.
- **Pass 2 (INFORMATIONAL):** Run remaining categories below. Lower severity but still actioned.
- **Specialist categories (handled by parallel subagents, NOT this checklist):** Test Gaps, Dead Code, Magic Numbers, Conditional Side Effects, Performance & Bundle Impact, Crypto & Entropy. See `review/specialists/` for these.

All findings get action via Fix-First Review: obvious mechanical fixes are applied automatically,
genuinely ambiguous issues are batched into a single user question.

**Output format:**

```
Pre-Landing Review: N issues (X critical, Y informational)

**AUTO-FIXED:**
- [file:line] Problem → fix applied

**NEEDS INPUT:**
- [file:line] Problem description
  Recommended fix: suggested fix
```

If no issues found: `Pre-Landing Review: No issues found.`

Be terse. For each issue: one line describing the problem, one line with the fix. No preamble, no summaries, no "looks good overall."

---

## Review Categories

### Pass 1 — CRITICAL

#### SQL & Data Safety
- String interpolation in SQL (even if values are `.to_i`/`.to_f` — use parameterized queries (Rails: sanitize_sql_array/Arel; Node: prepared statements; Python: parameterized queries))
- TOCTOU races: check-then-set patterns that should be atomic `WHERE` + `update_all`
- Bypassing model validations for direct DB writes (Rails: update_column; Django: QuerySet.update(); Prisma: raw queries)
- N+1 queries: Missing eager loading (Rails: .includes(); SQLAlchemy: joinedload(); Prisma: include) for associations used in loops/views

#### Race Conditions & Concurrency
- Read-check-write without uniqueness constraint or catch duplicate key error and retry (e.g., `where(hash:).first` then `save!` without handling concurrent insert)
- find-or-create without unique DB index — concurrent calls can create duplicates
- Status transitions that don't use atomic `WHERE old_status = ? UPDATE SET new_status` — concurrent updates can skip or double-apply transitions
- Unsafe HTML rendering (Rails: .html_safe/raw(); React: dangerouslySetInnerHTML; Vue: v-html; Django: |safe/mark_safe) on user-controlled data (XSS)

#### LLM Output Trust Boundary
- LLM-generated values (emails, URLs, names) written to DB or passed to mailers without format validation. Add lightweight guards (`EMAIL_REGEXP`, `URI.parse`, `.strip`) before persisting.
- Structured tool output (arrays, hashes) accepted without type/shape checks before database writes.
- LLM-generated URLs fetched without allowlist — SSRF risk if URL points to internal network (Python: `urllib.parse.urlparse` → check hostname against blocklist before `requests.get`/`httpx.get`)
- LLM output stored in knowledge bases or vector DBs without sanitization — stored prompt injection risk

#### Shell Injection (Python-specific)
- `subprocess.run()` / `subprocess.call()` / `subprocess.Popen()` with `shell=True` AND f-string/`.format()` interpolation in the command string — use argument arrays instead
- `os.system()` with variable interpolation — replace with `subprocess.run()` using argument arrays
- `eval()` / `exec()` on LLM-generated code without sandboxing

#### Enum & Value Completeness
When the diff introduces a new enum value, status string, tier name, or type constant:
- **Trace it through every consumer.** Read (don't just grep — READ) each file that switches on, filters by, or displays that value. If any consumer doesn't handle the new value, flag it. Common miss: adding a value to the frontend dropdown but the backend model/compute method doesn't persist it.
- **Check allowlists/filter arrays.** Search for arrays or `%w[]` lists containing sibling values (e.g., if adding "revise" to tiers, find every `%w[quick lfg mega]` and verify "revise" is included where needed).
- **Check `case`/`if-elsif` chains.** If existing code branches on the enum, does the new value fall through to a wrong default?
To do this: use Grep to find all references to the sibling values (e.g., grep for "lfg" or "mega" to find all tier consumers). Read each match. This step requires reading code OUTSIDE the diff.

### Pass 2 — INFORMATIONAL

#### Async/Sync Mixing (Python-specific)
- Synchronous `subprocess.run()`, `open()`, `requests.get()` inside `async def` endpoints — blocks the event loop. Use `asyncio.to_thread()`, `aiofiles`, or `httpx.AsyncClient` instead.
- `time.sleep()` inside async functions — use `asyncio.sleep()`
- Sync DB calls in async context without `run_in_executor()` wrapping

#### Column/Field Name Safety
- Verify column names in ORM queries (`.select()`, `.eq()`, `.gte()`, `.order()`) against actual DB schema — wrong column names silently return empty results or throw swallowed errors
- Check `.get()` calls on query results use the column name that was actually selected
- Cross-reference with schema documentation when available

#### Dead Code & Consistency (version/changelog only — other items handled by maintainability specialist)
- Version mismatch between PR title and VERSION/CHANGELOG files
- CHANGELOG entries that describe changes inaccurately (e.g., "changed from X to Y" when X never existed)

#### LLM Prompt Issues
- 0-indexed lists in prompts (LLMs reliably return 1-indexed)
- Prompt text listing available tools/capabilities that don't match what's actually wired up in the `tool_classes`/`tools` array
- Word/token limits stated in multiple places that could drift

#### Completeness Gaps
- Shortcut implementations where the complete version would cost <30 minutes CC time (e.g., partial enum handling, incomplete error paths, missing edge cases that are straightforward to add)
- Options presented with only human-team effort estimates — should show both human and CC+gstack time
- Test coverage gaps where adding the missing tests is a "lake" not an "ocean" (e.g., missing negative-path tests, missing edge case tests that mirror happy-path structure)
- Features implemented at 80-90% when 100% is achievable with modest additional code

#### Time Window Safety
- Date-key lookups that assume "today" covers 24h — report at 8am PT only sees midnight→8am under today's key
- Mismatched time windows between related features — one uses hourly buckets, another uses daily keys for the same data

#### Type Coercion at Boundaries
- Values crossing Ruby→JSON→JS boundaries where type could change (numeric vs string) — hash/digest inputs must normalize types
- Hash/digest inputs that don't call `.to_s` or equivalent before serialization — `{ cores: 8 }` vs `{ cores: "8" }` produce different hashes

#### View/Frontend
- Inline `<style>` blocks in partials (re-parsed every render)
- O(n*m) lookups in views (`Array#find` in a loop instead of `index_by` hash)
- Ruby-side `.select{}` filtering on DB results that could be a `WHERE` clause (unless intentionally avoiding leading-wildcard `LIKE`)

#### Distribution & CI/CD Pipeline
- CI/CD workflow changes (`.github/workflows/`): verify build tool versions match project requirements, artifact names/paths are correct, secrets use `${{ secrets.X }}` not hardcoded values
- New artifact types (CLI binary, library, package): verify a publish/release workflow exists and targets correct platforms
- Cross-platform builds: verify CI matrix covers all target OS/arch combinations, or documents which are untested
- Version tag format consistency: `v1.2.3` vs `1.2.3` — must match across VERSION file, git tags, and publish scripts
- Publish step idempotency: re-running the publish workflow should not fail (e.g., `gh release delete` before `gh release create`)

**DO NOT flag:**
- Web services with existing auto-deploy pipelines (Docker build + K8s deploy)
- Internal tools not distributed outside the team
- Test-only CI changes (adding test steps, not publish steps)

---

## Severity Classification

```
CRITICAL (highest severity):      INFORMATIONAL (main agent):      SPECIALIST (parallel subagents):
├─ SQL & Data Safety              ├─ Async/Sync Mixing             ├─ Testing specialist
├─ Race Conditions & Concurrency  ├─ Column/Field Name Safety      ├─ Maintainability specialist
├─ LLM Output Trust Boundary      ├─ Dead Code (version only)      ├─ Security specialist
├─ Shell Injection                ├─ LLM Prompt Issues             ├─ Performance specialist
└─ Enum & Value Completeness      ├─ Completeness Gaps             ├─ Data Migration specialist
                                   ├─ Time Window Safety            ├─ API Contract specialist
                                   ├─ Type Coercion at Boundaries   └─ Red Team (conditional)
                                   ├─ View/Frontend
                                   └─ Distribution & CI/CD Pipeline

All findings are actioned via Fix-First Review. Severity determines
presentation order and classification of AUTO-FIX vs ASK — critical
findings lean toward ASK (they're riskier), informational findings
lean toward AUTO-FIX (they're more mechanical).
```

---

## Fix-First Heuristic

This heuristic is referenced by both `/review` and `/ship`. It determines whether
the agent auto-fixes a finding or asks the user.

```
AUTO-FIX (agent fixes without asking):     ASK (needs human judgment):
├─ Dead code / unused variables            ├─ Security (auth, XSS, injection)
├─ N+1 queries (missing eager loading)      ├─ Race conditions
├─ Stale comments contradicting code       ├─ Design decisions
├─ Magic numbers → named constants         ├─ Large fixes (>20 lines)
├─ Missing LLM output validation           ├─ Enum completeness
├─ Version/path mismatches                 ├─ Removing functionality
├─ Variables assigned but never read       └─ Anything changing user-visible
└─ Inline styles, O(n*m) view lookups        behavior
```

**Rule of thumb:** If the fix is mechanical and a senior engineer would apply it
without discussion, it's AUTO-FIX. If reasonable engineers could disagree about
the fix, it's ASK.

**Critical findings default toward ASK** (they're inherently riskier).
**Informational findings default toward AUTO-FIX** (they're more mechanical).

---

## Suppressions — DO NOT flag these

- "X is redundant with Y" when the redundancy is harmless and aids readability (e.g., `present?` redundant with `length > 20`)
- "Add a comment explaining why this threshold/constant was chosen" — thresholds change during tuning, comments rot
- "This assertion could be tighter" when the assertion already covers the behavior
- Suggesting consistency-only changes (wrapping a value in a conditional to match how another constant is guarded)
- "Regex doesn't handle edge case X" when the input is constrained and X never occurs in practice
- "Test exercises multiple guards simultaneously" — that's fine, tests don't need to isolate every guard
- Eval threshold changes (max_actionable, min scores) — these are tuned empirically and change constantly
- Harmless no-ops (e.g., `.reject` on an element that's never in the array)
- ANYTHING already addressed in the diff you're reviewing — read the FULL diff before commenting

new file mode 100644

+import type { AgentConnectionEnvelope } from "./agent-onboarding-contract.js";
+
+export const BASE32_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+
+export const TOKEN_VERSION = "A";
+export const TOKEN_PREFIX = `CSWARM${TOKEN_VERSION}`;
+export const TOKEN_MARKER = `${TOKEN_PREFIX}.`;
+
+export function base32Encode(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  let bits = 0;
+  let value = 0;
+  let out = "";
+  for (const b of bytes) {
+    value = (value << 8) | b;
+    bits += 8;
+    while (bits >= 5) {
+      out += BASE32_ALPHABET[(value >>> (bits - 5)) & 31];
+      bits -= 5;
+    }
+  }
+  if (bits > 0) {
+    out += BASE32_ALPHABET[(value << (5 - bits)) & 31];
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+export function base32Decode(s: string): Uint8Array {
+  let bits = 0;
+  let value = 0;
+  const out: number[] = [];
+  for (const c of s) {
+    const i = BASE32_ALPHABET.indexOf(c);
+    if (i < 0) continue;
+    value = (value << 5) | i;
+    bits += 5;
+    if (bits >= 8) {
+      out.push((value >>> (bits - 8)) & 255);
+      bits -= 8;
+    }
+  }
+  return Uint8Array.from(out);
+}
+
+export function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
+  let c = ~0;
+  for (const x of bytes) {
+    c ^= x;
+    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
+      c = (c >>> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320 & -(c & 1));
+    }
+  }
+  return (~c) >>> 0;
+}
+
+export function crc32Bytes(crc: number): Uint8Array {
+  return Uint8Array.from([24, 16, 8, 0].map((shift) => (crc >>> shift) & 255));
+}
+
+export function normalizeTokenCandidate(raw: string): string {
+  return raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z2-7.]/g, "");
+}
+
+export function isAgentConnectionToken(raw: string): boolean {
+  if (typeof raw !== "string") return false;
+  const trimmed = raw.trim();
+  if (trimmed.startsWith("{")) return false;
+  const cleaned = normalizeTokenCandidate(trimmed);
+  return cleaned.includes(TOKEN_MARKER);
+}
+
+export function encodeAgentConnectionToken(
+  envelope: AgentConnectionEnvelope | Record<string, unknown> | string,
+): string {
+  const json = typeof envelope === "string" ? envelope : JSON.stringify(envelope);
+  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json);
+  const crc = crc32(bytes);
+  const crcB = crc32Bytes(crc);
+  return `${TOKEN_MARKER}${base32Encode(bytes)}.${base32Encode(crcB)}`;
+}

     1	import type { AgentConnectionEnvelope } from "./agent-onboarding-contract.js";
     2	
     3	export const BASE32_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
     4	
     5	export const TOKEN_VERSION = "A";
     6	export const TOKEN_PREFIX = `CSWARM${TOKEN_VERSION}`;
     7	export const TOKEN_MARKER = `${TOKEN_PREFIX}.`;
     8	
     9	export function base32Encode(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    10	  let bits = 0;
    11	  let value = 0;
    12	  let out = "";
    13	  for (const b of bytes) {
    14	    value = (value << 8) | b;
    15	    bits += 8;
    16	    while (bits >= 5) {
    17	      out += BASE32_ALPHABET[(value >>> (bits - 5)) & 31];
    18	      bits -= 5;
    19	    }
    20	  }
    21	  if (bits > 0) {
    22	    out += BASE32_ALPHABET[(value << (5 - bits)) & 31];
    23	  }
    24	  return out;
    25	}
    26	
    27	export function base32Decode(s: string): Uint8Array {
    28	  let bits = 0;
    29	  let value = 0;
    30	  const out: number[] = [];
    31	  for (const c of s) {
    32	    const i = BASE32_ALPHABET.indexOf(c);
    33	    if (i < 0) continue;
    34	    value = (value << 5) | i;
    35	    bits += 5;
    36	    if (bits >= 8) {
    37	      out.push((value >>> (bits - 8)) & 255);
    38	      bits -= 8;
    39	    }
    40	  }
    41	  return Uint8Array.from(out);
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
    45	  let c = ~0;
    46	  for (const x of bytes) {
    47	    c ^= x;
    48	    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
    49	      c = (c >>> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320 & -(c & 1));
    50	    }
    51	  }
    52	  return (~c) >>> 0;
    53	}
    54	
    55	export function crc32Bytes(crc: number): Uint8Array {
    56	  return Uint8Array.from([24, 16, 8, 0].map((shift) => (crc >>> shift) & 255));
    57	}
    58	
    59	export function normalizeTokenCandidate(raw: string): string {
    60	  return raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z2-7.]/g, "");
    61	}
    62	
    63	export function isAgentConnectionToken(raw: string): boolean {
    64	  if (typeof raw !== "string") return false;
    65	  const trimmed = raw.trim();
    66	  if (trimmed.startsWith("{")) return false;
    67	  const cleaned = normalizeTokenCandidate(trimmed);
    68	  return cleaned.includes(TOKEN_MARKER);
    69	}
    70	
    71	export function encodeAgentConnectionToken(
    72	  envelope: AgentConnectionEnvelope | Record<string, unknown> | string,
    73	): string {
    74	  const json = typeof envelope === "string" ? envelope : JSON.stringify(envelope);
    75	  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json);
    76	  const crc = crc32(bytes);
    77	  const crcB = crc32Bytes(crc);
    78	  return `${TOKEN_MARKER}${base32Encode(bytes)}.${base32Encode(crcB)}`;
    79	}

    29	  base32Encode,
    30	  base32Decode,
    31	  crc32,
    32	  crc32Bytes,
    33	  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
    34	  normalizeTokenCandidate,
    35	  isAgentConnectionToken,
    36	};
    37	
    38	const REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE =
    39	  "Use ‘Use a setup file’ in CommonSwarm and run setup with that file. Do not edit credentials or paste them into chat.";
    40	
    41	function checkedTarget(url: string, anonKey: string): CloudTarget {
    42	  try {
    43	    const target = cloudTarget(url, anonKey);
    44	    const parsed = new URL(target.url);
    45	    if (parsed.protocol !== "https:" && !["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "[::1]"].includes(parsed.hostname)) throw new Error();
    46	    if (anonKey.length > 4096 || /[\u0000-\u0020\u007f]/.test(anonKey)) throw new Error();
    47	    return target;
    48	  } catch {
    49	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_target_invalid", "Use an HTTPS deployment origin and its public key. HTTP is allowed only for local tests.");
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function validateAgentConnectionEnvelope(
    54	  value: unknown,
    55	): AgentConnectionEnvelope {
    56	  if (
    57	    !value ||
    58	    Array.isArray(value) ||
    59	    typeof value !== "object" ||
    60	    (value as Record<string, unknown>).version !== AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION ||
    61	    Object.keys(value).length !== AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.length ||
    62	    AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.some((key) => !Object.hasOwn(value as Record<string, unknown>, key)) ||
    63	    typeof (value as Record<string, unknown>).url !== "string" ||
    64	    typeof (value as Record<string, unknown>).anon_key !== "string" ||
    65	    typeof (value as Record<string, unknown>).workspace_id !== "string" ||
    66	    !ONBOARDING_UUID.test((value as Record<string, unknown>).workspace_id as string) ||
    67	    typeof (value as Record<string, unknown>).principal_id !== "string" ||
    68	    !ONBOARDING_UUID.test((value as Record<string, unknown>).principal_id as string) ||
    69	    !(value as Record<string, unknown>).credential ||
    70	    typeof (value as Record<string, unknown>).credential !== "object" ||
    71	    Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>).credential)
    72	  ) {
    73	    throw new AgentSetupError(
    74	      "connection_invalid",
    75	      `Expected connection version ${AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION} with fields: ${AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.join(", ")}. Save the supplied file unchanged.`,
    76	    );
    77	  }
    78	  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    79	  if (/^\s*\[[\s\S]*\]\(/.test(obj.url as string)) {
    80	    throw new AgentSetupError(
    81	      "connection_target_invalid",
    82	      `The connection URL appears to be a Markdown link. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
    83	    );
    84	  }
    85	  const target = checkedTarget(obj.url as string, obj.anon_key as string);
    86	  const agent = parseAgentCredentialInput(JSON.stringify(obj.credential), {
    87	    kind: "stdin",
    88	  });
    89	  if (
    90	    !agent.durable ||
    91	    agent.principalId !== (obj.principal_id as string).toLowerCase()
    92	  ) {
    93	    throw new AgentSetupError(
    94	      "connection_identity_mismatch",
    95	      "The connection and credential name different agents. Ask for a new connection file.",
    96	    );
    97	  }
    98	  return {
    99	    version: AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION,
   100	    url: target.url,
   101	    anon_key: target.anonKey,
   102	    workspace_id: (obj.workspace_id as string).toLowerCase(),
   103	    principal_id: agent.principalId,
   104	    credential: obj.credential as Record<string, unknown>,
   105	  };
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw: string): AgentConnectionEnvelope {
   109	  if (typeof raw !== "string") {
   110	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   111	      "token_marker_missing",
   112	      `The connection token is missing its marker. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   113	    );
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  const markerRegex =
   117	    /C[\s\*\_\\\`]*S[\s\*\_\\\`]*W[\s\*\_\\\`]*A[\s\*\_\\\`]*R[\s\*\_\\\`]*M[\s\*\_\\\`]*A[\s\*\_\\\`]*\./i;
   118	  const match = raw.match(markerRegex);
   119	  if (!match || match.index === undefined) {
   120	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   121	      "token_marker_missing",
   122	      `The connection token is missing its marker. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   123	    );
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  const markerStart = match.index;
   127	  const afterMarker = raw.slice(markerStart + match[0].length);
   128	
   129	  let bodyChars = "";
   130	  let crcChars = "";
   131	  let inCrc = false;
   132	  let crcBase32Count = 0;
   133	  let tokenEnd = -1;
   134	  const extraParts: string[] = [];
   135	
   136	  for (let i = 0; i < afterMarker.length; i++) {
   137	    const ch = afterMarker[i]!;
   138	    if (ch === ".") {
   139	      if (!inCrc) {
   140	        inCrc = true;
   141	        continue;
   142	      } else {
   143	        extraParts.push("");
   144	        continue;
   145	      }
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    const upper = ch.toUpperCase();
   149	    const isBase32 = BASE32_ALPHABET.includes(upper);
   150	
   151	    if (extraParts.length > 0) {
   152	      if (isBase32) {
   153	        extraParts[extraParts.length - 1] += upper;
   154	      }
   155	      continue;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    if (!inCrc) {
   159	      if (isBase32) {
   160	        bodyChars += upper;
   161	      }
   162	    } else {
   163	      if (isBase32) {
   164	        crcChars += upper;
   165	        crcBase32Count++;
   166	        if (crcBase32Count === 7) {
   167	          let nextNonFormat: string | null = null;
   168	          for (let j = i + 1; j < afterMarker.length; j++) {
   169	            const cj = afterMarker[j]!;
   170	            if (/[\*\_\\\`]/.test(cj)) continue;
   171	            nextNonFormat = cj;
   172	            break;
   173	          }
   174	          if (nextNonFormat === null || /[\s\)\]\>\'\"\`]/.test(nextNonFormat)) {
   175	            tokenEnd = markerStart + match[0].length + i + 1;
   176	            break;
   177	          }
   178	        }
   179	      }
   180	    }
   181	  }
   182	
   183	  let parts: string[];
   184	  if (tokenEnd !== -1 && extraParts.length === 0) {
   185	    parts = [TOKEN_PREFIX, bodyChars, crcChars];
   186	  } else {
   187	    if (extraParts.length > 0) {
   188	      parts = [TOKEN_PREFIX, bodyChars, crcChars, ...extraParts];
   189	    } else {
   190	      parts = [TOKEN_PREFIX, bodyChars, crcChars];
   191	    }
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  if (
   195	    parts.length !== 3 ||
   196	    parts[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX ||
   197	    parts[1].length === 0 ||
   198	    parts[2].length === 0
   199	  ) {
   200	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   201	      "token_shape_invalid",
   202	      `The connection token format is invalid. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   203	    );
   204	  }
   205	
   206	  if (parts[2].length !== 7) {
   207	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   208	      "token_checksum_invalid",
   209	      `The connection token checksum did not match. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   210	    );
   211	  }
   212	
   213	  const body = base32Decode(parts[1]);
   214	  const want = base32Decode(parts[2]);
   215	
   216	  if (want.length !== 4) {
   217	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   218	      "token_checksum_invalid",
   219	      `The connection token checksum did not match. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   220	    );
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  if (base32Encode(want) !== parts[2] || base32Encode(body) !== parts[1]) {
   224	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   225	      "token_checksum_invalid",
   226	      `The connection token checksum did not match. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   227	    );
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  const got = crc32(body);
   231	  const wantN =
   232	    ((want[0]! << 24) | (want[1]! << 16) | (want[2]! << 8) | want[3]!) >>> 0;
   233	  if (got !== wantN) {
   234	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   235	      "token_checksum_invalid",
   236	      `The connection token checksum did not match. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   237	    );
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  let jsonString: string;
   241	  try {
   242	    jsonString = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(body);
   243	  } catch {
   244	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   245	      "token_payload_invalid",
   246	      `The connection token payload is damaged. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   247	    );
   248	  }
   249	  let parsed: unknown;
   250	  try {
   251	    parsed = JSON.parse(jsonString);
   252	  } catch {
   253	    throw new AgentSetupError(
   254	      "token_payload_invalid",
   255	      `The connection token payload is damaged. ${REPAIR_USE_SETUP_FILE}`,
   256	    );
   257	  }
   258	  return validateAgentConnectionEnvelope(parsed);
   259	}

    28	export interface AgentProfile {
    29	  version: 1;
    30	  url: string;
    31	  anon_key: string;
    32	  workspace_id: string;
    33	  principal_id: string;
    34	  credential_file: string;

    36	
    37	export function privatePath(path: string): string {
    38	  if (path.startsWith("~/")) path = join(homedir(), path.slice(2));
    39	  if (!isAbsolute(path) || /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(path)) {
    40	    throw new AgentSetupError("profile_path_invalid", "Use an absolute private file path outside a repository.");
    41	  }
    42	  return resolve(path);
    43	}
    44	
    45	/** Check each existing ancestor, including repos reached through an ancestor symlink. */
    46	export async function assertPrivateLocation(path: string): Promise<string> {
    47	  const absolute = privatePath(path);
    48	  let current = dirname(absolute);
    49	  while (true) {
    50	    try {
    51	      const info = await lstat(current);
    52	      if (info.isSymbolicLink()) {
    53	        // macOS /tmp and /var are system aliases. Resolve them, then inspect the real tree.
    54	        const resolved = await realpath(current);
    55	        if (current !== "/tmp" && current !== "/var") {
    56	          throw new AgentSetupError("profile_symlink", "A private state path must not pass through a symlink.");
    57	        }
    58	        await assertPrivateLocation(join(resolved, "probe"));
    59	      }
    60	      try {
    61	        await lstat(join(current, ".git"));
    62	        throw new AgentSetupError("profile_inside_repository", "Keep the connection file and agent profile outside repositories.");
    63	      } catch (error) {
    64	        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
    65	      }
    66	    } catch (error) {
    67	      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
    68	    }
    69	    const parent = dirname(current);
    70	    if (parent === current) break;
    71	    current = parent;
    72	  }
    73	  return absolute;
    74	}
    75	
    76	export { isAgentConnectionToken, decodeAgentConnectionToken, encodeAgentConnectionToken };
    77	
    78	export function parseAgentConnection(raw: string): AgentConnectionEnvelope {
    79	  if (isAgentConnectionToken(raw)) {
    80	    return decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw);
    81	  }
    82	  let value: Record<string, unknown>;
    83	  try { value = JSON.parse(raw); } catch {
    84	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_invalid", raw.includes("\\_") || /^\s*```/.test(raw)
    85	      ? "The connection file appears to contain Markdown formatting. Use ‘Use a setup file’ in CommonSwarm and run setup with that file. Do not edit credentials or paste them into chat."
    86	      : "The connection file is not valid JSON. Use ‘Use a setup file’ in CommonSwarm and run setup with that file. Do not paste its contents into chat.");
    87	  }
    88	  if (!value || Array.isArray(value) || typeof value !== "object" ||
    89	      value.version !== AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION ||
    90	      Object.keys(value).length !== AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.length ||
    91	      AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.some(key => !Object.hasOwn(value, key)) ||
    92	      typeof value.url !== "string" || typeof value.anon_key !== "string" ||
    93	      typeof value.workspace_id !== "string" || !ONBOARDING_UUID.test(value.workspace_id) ||
    94	      typeof value.principal_id !== "string" || !ONBOARDING_UUID.test(value.principal_id) ||
    95	      !value.credential || typeof value.credential !== "object" || Array.isArray(value.credential)) {
    96	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_invalid", `Expected connection version ${AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION} with fields: ${AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS.join(", ")}. Save the supplied file unchanged.`);
    97	  }
    98	  if (/^\s*\[[\s\S]*\]\(/.test(value.url)) {
    99	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_target_invalid", "The connection URL appears to be a Markdown link. Use ‘Use a setup file’ in CommonSwarm and run setup with that file. Do not edit credentials or paste them into chat.");
   100	  }
   101	  const target = checkedTarget(value.url, value.anon_key);
   102	  const agent = parseAgentCredentialInput(JSON.stringify(value.credential), { kind: "stdin" });
   103	  if (!agent.durable || agent.principalId !== value.principal_id.toLowerCase()) {
   104	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_identity_mismatch", "The connection and credential name different agents. Ask for a new connection file.");
   105	  }
   106	  return {
   107	    version: AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION, url: target.url, anon_key: target.anonKey,
   108	    workspace_id: value.workspace_id.toLowerCase(), principal_id: agent.principalId,
   109	    credential: value.credential as Record<string, unknown>,
   110	  };
   111	}
   112	
   113	function checkedTarget(url: string, anonKey: string): CloudTarget {
   114	  try {
   115	    const target = cloudTarget(url, anonKey);
   116	    const parsed = new URL(target.url);
   117	    if (parsed.protocol !== "https:" && !["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "[::1]"].includes(parsed.hostname)) throw new Error();
   118	    if (anonKey.length > 4096 || /[\u0000-\u0020\u007f]/.test(anonKey)) throw new Error();
   119	    return target;
   120	  } catch {
   121	    throw new AgentSetupError("connection_target_invalid", "Use an HTTPS deployment origin and its public key. HTTP is allowed only for local tests.");
   122	  }
   123	}
   124	
   125	export function defaultAgentProfilePath(connection: Pick<AgentProfile, "url" | "anon_key" | "workspace_id" | "principal_id">): string {
   126	  const target = checkedTarget(connection.url, connection.anon_key);
   127	  return join(homedir(), ".cswarm", "agents", target.profileId, connection.workspace_id, connection.principal_id, "profile.json");
   128	}
   129	
   130	export async function readAgentProfile(path: string): Promise<AgentProfile> {
   131	  path = await assertPrivateLocation(path);
   132	  const raw = await readSecureJsonFileIfPresent(path, ONBOARDING_MAX_FILE_BYTES);
   133	  if (raw === null) throw new AgentSetupError("profile_missing", "The agent profile is missing. Run cswarm setup with the connection file.");
   134	  let p: AgentProfile;
   135	  try { p = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { throw new AgentSetupError("profile_invalid", "The agent profile is damaged. Run setup again."); }
   136	  if (!p || p.version !== 1 || Object.keys(p).sort().join() !== ["version", "url", "anon_key", "workspace_id", "principal_id", "credential_file"].sort().join() ||
   137	      typeof p.url !== "string" || typeof p.anon_key !== "string" ||
   138	      typeof p.workspace_id !== "string" || !ONBOARDING_UUID.test(p.workspace_id) ||
   139	      typeof p.principal_id !== "string" || !ONBOARDING_UUID.test(p.principal_id) ||
   140	      p.credential_file !== join(dirname(path), "credential.json")) {
   141	    throw new AgentSetupError("profile_invalid", "The agent profile is damaged. Run setup again.");
   142	  }
   143	  checkedTarget(p.url, p.anon_key);
   144	  return p;
   145	}
   146	
   147	export async function readProfileCredential(profile: AgentProfile): Promise<AgentCredentialInput> {
   148	  const raw = await readSecureJsonFileIfPresent(profile.credential_file, ONBOARDING_MAX_FILE_BYTES);
   149	  if (raw === null) throw new AgentSetupError("profile_credential_missing", "The profile credential is missing. Run setup again.");
   150	  const agent = parseAgentCredentialInput(raw, { kind: "file", path: profile.credential_file });
   151	  if (agent.principalId !== profile.principal_id) throw new AgentSetupError("profile_identity_mismatch", "The saved credential belongs to a different agent. Run setup again.");
   152	  return agent;
   153	}
   154	
   155	export async function openProfileCredential(profile: AgentProfile, fetcher: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<AgentCredentialSession> {
   156	  const agent = await readProfileCredential(profile);
   157	  const target = checkedTarget(profile.url, profile.anon_key);
   158	  const store = await agentCredentialStore({ target, lineageKey: credentialLineageKey(agent.token) });
   159	  return AgentCredentialSession.open({ target, workspaceId: profile.workspace_id, presented: agent, store, fetcher });
   160	}
   161	
   162	export async function saveAgentProfile(path: string, connection: AgentConnectionEnvelope): Promise<AgentProfile> {
   163	  path = await assertPrivateLocation(path);
   164	  const profile: AgentProfile = {
   165	    version: 1, url: connection.url, anon_key: connection.anon_key,
   166	    workspace_id: connection.workspace_id, principal_id: connection.principal_id,
   167	    credential_file: join(dirname(path), "credential.json"),
   168	  };
   169	  await withFileLock(dirname(path), "setup", async () => {
   170	    const existingRaw = await readSecureJsonFileIfPresent(path, ONBOARDING_MAX_FILE_BYTES);
   171	    if (existingRaw !== null) {
   172	      const existing = await readAgentProfile(path);
   173	      if (existing.url !== profile.url || existing.workspace_id !== profile.workspace_id || existing.principal_id !== profile.principal_id) {
   174	        throw new AgentSetupError("profile_conflict", "This profile belongs to another workspace or agent. Use a different profile path.");
   175	      }
   176	    }
   177	    await writeSecureJsonFile(profile.credential_file, JSON.stringify(connection.credential));
   178	    await writeSecureJsonFile(path, JSON.stringify(profile));
   179	  });
   180	  return profile;
   181	}
   182	
   183	export function profileScopeKey(hostSessionId?: string): string {
   184	  return hostSessionId === undefined ? "manual" : createHash("sha256").update(hostSessionId).digest("hex");
   185	}
   186	
   187	export function profileTarget(profile: AgentProfile): CloudTarget {
   188	  return checkedTarget(profile.url, profile.anon_key);
   189	}
   190	
   191	/** A supplied host ID may select its own managed proof; never take another session's proof. */
   192	export async function profileSessionContext(profile: AgentProfile, hostSessionId?: string) {
   193	  if (hostSessionId === undefined || hostSessionId === "manual") return null;
   194	  const contexts = (await listSessionContexts(profile.workspace_id, profile.principal_id)).filter(c => sessionProofOf(c) !== null);
   195	  if (contexts.length === 0) return null;
   196	  const matches = contexts.filter(c => c.host_session_id === hostSessionId);
   197	  if (matches.length !== 1) throw new AgentSetupError("profile_session_conflict", "This agent has no single managed context for this host session. Check cswarm session status; do not use another session's context.");
   198	  const context = matches[0]!;
   199	  assertLocalSessionBinding(context, {
   200	    target: profileTarget(profile), tokenPrincipalId: profile.principal_id,
   201	    flagWorkspaceId: profile.workspace_id, tokenFile: profile.credential_file, hostSessionId,
   202	  });
   203	  return { context, path: defaultSessionContextPath(profile.workspace_id, profile.principal_id, context.session_id) };
   204	}

    49	function artifact(principal = AGENT) {
    50	  return {
    51	    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
    52	    status: "accepted",
    53	    principal_id: principal,
    54	    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
    55	    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
    56	    agent_token: TOKEN,
    57	    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    58	  };
    59	}
    60	
    61	function connection(url = "https://fixture.example", principal = AGENT): AgentConnectionEnvelope {
    62	  return {
    63	    version: AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION,
    64	    url,
    65	    anon_key: "public-fixture",
    66	    workspace_id: WS,
    67	    principal_id: principal,
    68	    credential: artifact(principal),
    69	  };
    70	}
    71	
    72	function mockFetcher(principal = AGENT) {
    73	  const requests: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    74	  const fetcher = (async (_input: unknown, init?: RequestInit) => {
    75	    const body = JSON.parse(String(init?.body));
    76	    requests.push(body);
    77	    assert.equal(new Headers(init?.headers).get("authorization"), `Bearer ${TOKEN}`);
    78	    assert.equal(init?.redirect, "error", "credentials must not follow a redirect");
    79	    let result;
    80	    if (body.resource === "members") {
    81	      result = {
    82	        members: [{ user_id: OWNER, display_name: "Owner" }],
    83	        agents: [{ principal_id: principal, name: "Test agent", owner_user_id: OWNER }],
    84	        identity: { credential_valid: true, principal_id: principal, workspace_id: WS, owner_user_id: OWNER },
    85	      };
    86	    } else if (body.resource === "signals") {
    87	      result = {
    88	        signals: [],
    89	        capabilities: { sender_owner_relation: 1, cursor_after: 1 },
    90	      };
    91	    } else {
    92	      throw new Error(`unexpected request: ${body.resource}`);
    93	    }
    94	    return new Response(JSON.stringify(result), { status: 200 });
    95	  }) as typeof fetch;
    96	  return { fetcher, requests };
    97	}

    99	test("a token with underscores escaped as \\_ inserted (must succeed)", () => {
   100	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   101	  // A wrong implementation that fails to strip backslashes or fails to tolerate non-base32
   102	  // escaped characters (e.g. treating \`\\_\` as syntax errors or failing to strip backslashes
   103	  // before base32 decoding) would fail. To pass, an implementation must filter out all
   104	  // characters outside the base32 alphabet (A-Z, 2-7, and dot), ignoring backslashes and
   105	  // underscores introduced by Markdown processors.
   106	  const env = connection();
   107	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   108	
   109	  const hostileVariants = [
   110	    `\\_${token}`,
   111	    `${token}\\_`,
   112	    `${token.slice(0, 20)}\\_${token.slice(20)}`,
   113	    `${token.slice(0, 30)}\\_\\_${token.slice(30, 60)}\\_${token.slice(60)}`,
   114	    token.replace("CSWARMA.", "CSWARMA.\\_"),
   115	    token.replace("CSWARMA.", "CSWARMA._"),
   116	  ];
   117	
   118	  for (const variant of hostileVariants) {
   119	    const decoded = decodeAgentConnectionToken(variant);
   120	    assert.deepEqual(decoded, env);
   121	    assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(variant), true);
   122	    assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(variant), env);
   123	  }
   124	});

   153	test("a token wrapped in altered or malformed code fences (must succeed)", () => {
   154	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   155	  // A wrong implementation that relies on rigid code fence regular expressions,
   156	  // requires clean newlines around fences, or fails when fence language tags (like \`json\`)
   157	  // are glued directly to the prefix marker without whitespace, would fail to extract the token.
   158	  // To pass, an implementation must locate the \`CSWARMA.\` prefix marker even when preceded
   159	  // by arbitrary fence tags and strip backticks and surrounding markdown formatting.
   160	  const env = connection();
   161	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   162	
   163	  const fenceVariants = [
   164	    `\`\`\`json\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
   165	    `\`\`\`json\r\n${token}\r\n\`\`\``,
   166	    `\`\`\`\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
   167	    `\`\`json\n${token}\n\`\``,
   168	    `\`\`\`\`json\n${token}\n\`\`\`\``,
   169	    `\`\`\`json${token}\`\`\``, // language tag glued directly to token prefix
   170	    `\`\`json${token}\`\``,
   171	    `\`${token}\``,
   172	    `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
   173	    `~~~json\n${token}\n~~~`,
   174	  ];
   175	
   176	  for (const variant of fenceVariants) {
   177	    const decoded = decodeAgentConnectionToken(variant);
   178	    assert.deepEqual(decoded, env);
   179	    assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(variant), true);
   180	    assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(variant), env);
   181	  }

   184	test("a token broken across lines with spaces and tabs inserted (must SUCCEED: wrapping is recoverable)", () => {
   185	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   186	  // A wrong implementation that assumes the token is delivered on a single contiguous line,
   187	  // or considers whitespace (spaces, tabs, line breaks) as invalid base32 characters, would fail
   188	  // on tokens wrapped across lines by terminal windows or chat applications. To pass,
   189	  // the implementation must strip all whitespace characters and recover the contiguous base32 token.
   190	  const env = connection();
   191	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   192	
   193	  const wrappedVariants = [
   194	    token.match(/.{1,25}/g)!.join("\n"),
   195	    token.match(/.{1,30}/g)!.join("\r\n"),
   196	    token.match(/.{1,20}/g)!.join("\n  \t  "),
   197	    token.match(/.{1,40}/g)!.join("\t\n\t"),
   198	    `   \t\n${token.slice(0, 40)}\n   \t   ${token.slice(40, 100)}\r\n\t   ${token.slice(100)}   \n\t`,
   199	  ];
   200	
   201	  for (const variant of wrappedVariants) {
   202	    const decoded = decodeAgentConnectionToken(variant);
   203	    assert.deepEqual(decoded, env);
   204	    assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(variant), true);
   205	    assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(variant), env);
   206	  }
   207	});

   209	test("a lowercase token (must succeed)", () => {
   210	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   211	  // A wrong implementation that is strictly case-sensitive and only accepts uppercase
   212	  // RFC 4648 base32 letters (A-Z) would fail when input has been lowercased by a user
   213	  // or chat transformer. To pass, an implementation must normalize input with .toUpperCase()
   214	  // before matching the marker and decoding base32.
   215	  const env = connection();
   216	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   217	
   218	  const lower = token.toLowerCase();
   219	  assert.notEqual(lower, token);
   220	  assert.deepEqual(decodeAgentConnectionToken(lower), env);
   221	  assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(lower), true);
   222	  assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(lower), env);
   223	
   224	  const mixed = token
   225	    .split("")
   226	    .map((c, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? c.toLowerCase() : c.toUpperCase()))
   227	    .join("");
   228	  assert.deepEqual(decodeAgentConnectionToken(mixed), env);
   229	  assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(mixed), true);
   230	  assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(mixed), env);
   231	});

   233	test("a truncated token (must fail on the checksum, not on JSON)", () => {
   234	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   235	  // A wrong implementation that attempts to decode and parse JSON before validating
   236	  // the CRC32 checksum would fail with a JSON SyntaxError (e.g. "Unexpected end of JSON input")
   237	  // or a UTF-8 decoding error when given a truncated token, rather than detecting the corruption
   238	  // through the checksum. To pass, the implementation must verify the CRC32 checksum FIRST
   239	  // before attempting UTF-8 decoding or JSON parsing, throwing `token_checksum_invalid`.
   240	  const env = connection();
   241	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   242	
   243	  const originalJsonParse = JSON.parse;
   244	  let jsonParseCalls = 0;
   245	  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalJsonParse>) => {
   246	    jsonParseCalls++;
   247	    return originalJsonParse(...args);
   248	  };
   249	
   250	  try {
   251	    // 1. Cut 1 to 5 characters from the end of the token
   252	    for (let cut = 1; cut <= 5; cut++) {
   253	      const truncated = token.slice(0, -cut);
   254	      assert.throws(
   255	        () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(truncated),
   256	        (err: unknown) => {
   257	          assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   258	          assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   259	          assert.ok(!(err instanceof SyntaxError), "must not throw a JSON SyntaxError");
   260	          return true;
   261	        },
   262	      );
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    // 2. Cut characters from the body while keeping the checksum portion
   266	    const lastDot = token.lastIndexOf(".");
   267	    const prefixAndBody = token.slice(0, lastDot);
   268	    const checksumPart = token.slice(lastDot + 1);
   269	
   270	    for (let cut = 1; cut <= 5; cut++) {
   271	      const truncatedBody = `${prefixAndBody.slice(0, -cut)}.${checksumPart}`;
   272	      assert.throws(
   273	        () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(truncatedBody),
   274	        (err: unknown) => {
   275	          assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   276	          assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   277	          assert.ok(!(err instanceof SyntaxError), "must not throw a JSON SyntaxError");
   278	          return true;
   279	        },
   280	      );
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    // Prove checksum failure happened BEFORE JSON.parse or envelope validation was reached
   284	    assert.equal(jsonParseCalls, 0, "JSON.parse must never be reached for truncated tokens failing checksum");
   285	  } finally {
   286	    JSON.parse = originalJsonParse;
   287	  }
   288	});
   289	
   290	test("one flipped character (must fail on the checksum)", () => {
   291	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   292	  // A wrong implementation that lacks CRC32 checksum verification or ignores checksum
   293	  // mismatches would decode corrupted payload data and fail only later during JSON parsing
   294	  // or semantic validation. To pass, an implementation must compute the CRC32 over the
   295	  // decoded body and reject any mismatch with `token_checksum_invalid`.
   296	  const env = connection();
   297	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   298	
   299	  const originalJsonParse = JSON.parse;
   300	  let jsonParseCalls = 0;
   301	  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalJsonParse>) => {
   302	    jsonParseCalls++;
   303	    return originalJsonParse(...args);
   304	  };
   305	
   306	  try {
   307	    // Flip a character in the token body
   308	    const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
   309	    const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
   310	    const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
   311	    const flippedBodyChar = token[bodyMid] === "A" ? "B" : "A";
   312	    const flippedBodyToken =
   313	      token.slice(0, bodyMid) + flippedBodyChar + token.slice(bodyMid + 1);
   314	
   315	    assert.throws(
   316	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedBodyToken),
   317	      (err: unknown) => {
   318	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   319	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   320	        return true;
   321	      },
   322	    );
   323	
   324	    // Flip a character in the checksum portion
   325	    const crcStart = bodyEnd + 1;
   326	    const crcIdx = crcStart + 2;
   327	    const flippedCrcChar = token[crcIdx] === "A" ? "B" : "A";
   328	    const flippedCrcToken =
   329	      token.slice(0, crcIdx) + flippedCrcChar + token.slice(crcIdx + 1);
   330	
   331	    assert.throws(
   332	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedCrcToken),
   333	      (err: unknown) => {
   334	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   335	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   336	        return true;
   337	      },
   338	    );
   339	
   340	    // Flip the final body symbol (must fail canonical base32 check, not reach JSON)
   341	    const finalBodyIdx = bodyEnd - 1;
   342	    const flippedFinalBodyChar = token[finalBodyIdx] === "U" ? "V" : "U";
   343	    const flippedFinalBodyToken =
   344	      token.slice(0, finalBodyIdx) + flippedFinalBodyChar + token.slice(bodyEnd);
   345	
   346	    assert.throws(
   347	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedFinalBodyToken),
   348	      (err: unknown) => {
   349	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   350	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   351	        return true;
   352	      },
   353	    );
   354	
   355	    // Flip the final checksum symbol (must fail canonical base32 check, not reach JSON)
   356	    const finalCrcIdx = token.length - 1;
   357	    const flippedFinalCrcChar = token[finalCrcIdx] === "Q" ? "R" : "Q";
   358	    const flippedFinalCrcToken =
   359	      token.slice(0, finalCrcIdx) + flippedFinalCrcChar;
   360	
   361	    assert.throws(
   362	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedFinalCrcToken),
   363	      (err: unknown) => {
   364	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   365	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   366	        return true;
   367	      },
   368	    );
   369	
   370	    // Prove all checksum failures happened BEFORE JSON.parse or envelope validation was reached
   371	    assert.equal(jsonParseCalls, 0, "JSON.parse must never be reached for flipped characters failing checksum");
   372	  } finally {
   373	    JSON.parse = originalJsonParse;
   374	  }
   375	});

   377	test("an envelope whose credential is invalid (must fail in the existing validator, not the decoder)", () => {
   378	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   379	  // A wrong implementation that conflates token decoding with credential schema validation
   380	  // might throw a token decoder error (like `token_payload_invalid`) instead of delegating
   381	  // to the existing envelope and credential validators. Conversely, a wrong implementation
   382	  // that fails to execute the existing validator would accept malformed credentials.
   383	  // To pass, the token decoder must successfully decode the valid base32 envelope and pass
   384	  // it to `validateAgentConnectionEnvelope`, which preserves existing validator error codes.
   385	  const env = connection();
   386	
   387	  // Case 1: Principal mismatch between envelope principal_id and credential principal_id
   388	  const mismatchedEnvelope = {
   389	    ...env,
   390	    principal_id: OTHER,
   391	  };
   392	  const tokenMismatch = encodeAgentConnectionToken(mismatchedEnvelope);
   393	  assert.throws(
   394	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenMismatch),
   395	    (err: unknown) => {
   396	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   397	      assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "connection_identity_mismatch");
   398	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_payload_invalid");
   399	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   400	      return true;
   401	    },
   402	  );
   403	
   404	  // Case 2: Credential missing required agent_token
   405	  const invalidCredEnvelope = {
   406	    ...env,
   407	    credential: {
   408	      ...env.credential,
   409	      agent_token: undefined,
   410	    },
   411	  };
   412	  const tokenBadCred = encodeAgentConnectionToken(invalidCredEnvelope as unknown as AgentConnectionEnvelope);
   413	  assert.throws(
   414	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenBadCred),
   415	    (err: unknown) => {
   416	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentCredentialInputError, `expected AgentCredentialInputError but got ${err}`);
   417	      assert.equal(err.code, "agent_credential_missing_agent_token");
   418	      return true;
   419	    },
   420	  );
   421	
   422	  // Case 3: Credential is not an object
   423	  const nonObjectCredEnvelope = {
   424	    ...env,
   425	    credential: "not-an-object",
   426	  };
   427	  const tokenNonObj = encodeAgentConnectionToken(nonObjectCredEnvelope as unknown as AgentConnectionEnvelope);
   428	  assert.throws(
   429	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenNonObj),
   430	    (err: unknown) => {
   431	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   432	      assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "connection_invalid");
   433	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_payload_invalid");
   434	      return true;
   435	    },
   436	  );
   437	});

   439	test("a round trip: encode then decode returns the original bytes exactly", () => {
   440	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   441	  // A wrong implementation that re-serializes JSON with altered key order, modifies
   442	  // whitespace or character encoding, or introduces padding corruption during base32
   443	  // encoding/decoding would fail to preserve the exact byte representation of the payload.
   444	  // To pass, the token encoder and decoder must preserve the exact UTF-8 byte stream
   445	  // without losing, reordering, or adding any bytes.
   446	  const env = connection();
   447	  const rawJson = JSON.stringify(env);
   448	  const originalBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(rawJson);
   449	
   450	  // Encode the envelope
   451	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   452	
   453	  // Decode the token and assert envelope equality
   454	  const decoded = decodeAgentConnectionToken(token);
   455	  assert.deepEqual(decoded, env);
   456	
   457	  // Assert the UTF-8 JSON payload bytes match byte-for-byte
   458	  const parts = token.split(".");
   459	  assert.equal(parts.length, 3);
   460	  const payloadBytes = base32Decode(parts[1]!);
   461	  assert.deepEqual(payloadBytes, originalBytes);
   462	
   463	  // Assert TextDecoder recovers the exact original JSON string
   464	  const recoveredJson = new TextDecoder("utf-8").decode(payloadBytes);
   465	  assert.equal(recoveredJson, rawJson);
   466	
   467	  // Also verify encoding a direct string payload preserves exact bytes
   468	  const customJson = JSON.stringify(env, null, 2);
   469	  const customBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(customJson);
   470	  const tokenFromStr = encodeAgentConnectionToken(customJson);
   471	  const decodedFromStr = decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenFromStr);
   472	  assert.deepEqual(decodedFromStr, env);
   473	  const partsFromStr = tokenFromStr.split(".");
   474	  const payloadBytesFromStr = base32Decode(partsFromStr[1]!);
   475	  assert.deepEqual(payloadBytesFromStr, customBytes);
   476	});

   478	test("Control proving the OLD path still works: a plain JSON envelope file still sets up", async () => {
   479	  // 1. Write a valid plain JSON envelope to a private 0600 file in a 0700 dir.
   480	  // Call setupAgent with a mock/fake fetcher and verify setup succeeds.
   481	  const jsonDir = await mkdtemp(join(root, "json-setup-"));

   503	  await chmod(tokenDir, 0o700);

   511	    connectionFile: tokenFilePath,

   513	    fetcher: fake2.fetcher,
   514	  });

   519	  assert.equal(fake2.requests.length, 2);

   566	test("token_payload_invalid: valid checksum but malformed JSON throws token_payload_invalid", () => {
   567	  const nonJson = new TextEncoder().encode("not valid json at all");
   568	  const crc = crc32(nonJson);
   569	  const crcBytes = Uint8Array.from([24, 16, 8, 0].map((s) => (crc >>> s) & 255));
   570	  const badToken = `CSWARMA.${base32Encode(nonJson)}.${base32Encode(crcBytes)}`;
   571	
   572	  assert.throws(
   573	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(badToken),
   574	    (err: unknown) => {
   575	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   576	      assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_payload_invalid");
   577	      return true;
   578	    },
   579	  );
   580	});

     2	import { credentialArtifact, type AgentCredential } from "../../lib/agent-connect";
     3	import { SIGNAL_BODY_MAX } from "../../../../supabase/functions/_shared/signal-text";
     4	import { INSTALL_CMD } from "../../lib/install";
     5	import { AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION, type AgentConnectionEnvelope } from "../../../../src/cloud/agent-onboarding-contract";
     6	import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../../../src/cloud/agent-credential-input";
     7	import { encodeAgentConnectionToken } from "../../../../src/cloud/agent-connection-codec";

     9	export interface DashboardPromptInput {
    10	  credential: AgentCredential;
    11	  workspaceId: string;
    12	  workspaceName: string;
    13	  deploymentUrl: string;
    14	  anonKey: string;
    15	}
    16	
    17	export function dashboardAgentConnection(input: DashboardPromptInput): string {
    18	  const envelope: AgentConnectionEnvelope = {
    19	    version: AGENT_CONNECTION_VERSION,
    20	    url: input.deploymentUrl,
    21	    anon_key: input.anonKey,
    22	    workspace_id: input.workspaceId,
    23	    principal_id: input.credential.principalId,
    24	    credential: { ...JSON.parse(credentialArtifact(input.credential)), message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 },
    25	  };
    26	  return JSON.stringify(envelope);
    27	}
    28	
    29	function setupPrompt(source: string): string {
    30	  return [
    31	    "Connect this agent to CommonSwarm. Keep the connection file private; never echo its contents or put them in shell commands, logs, URLs, or environment variables.",
    32	    `Use Node.js 22+ and run:
    33	
    34	${codeBlock("sh", INSTALL_CMD)}`,
    35	    "The installer reuses a matching build. If its host is blocked, use npm install -g commonswarm. Confirm cswarm setup --check-version returns setup_version 1; otherwise report that the release needs updating.",
    36	    source,
    37	    `Use Markdown for messages (up to ${SIGNAL_BODY_MAX} characters).`,
    38	    "Run cswarm setup --connection-file <private-file> --json. Use the returned --profile with later commands.",
    39	    "Ask once: enable wakeups in this same session, or check at each turn's start and whenever asked? Wake requires Claude Code preview channels; Codex supports turn checks. Explain any approval or restart needed. Use cswarm receive configure with the user's choice; reuse a saved choice. Never start another model to answer here.",
    40	    "Run cswarm check before work. Read only relevant brain topics; post intent and reply to requests. Use cswarm setup guide only when needed. Report the connection, receive mode, and next step; do not claim wake works until its idle test passes.",
    41	  ].join("\n\n");
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function dashboardAgentFilePrompt(_input: DashboardPromptInput): string {
    45	  return setupPrompt("Import the attached connection JSON. Use your file-writing tool to save it outside repositories in a 0700 directory with file mode 0600.");
    46	}
    47	
    48	/** One-paste fallback: one credential and one copy of the public connection data. */
    49	export function dashboardAgentPrompt(input: DashboardPromptInput): string {
    50	  const path = `~/.cswarm/connect-${input.credential.principalId}/connection.json`;
    51	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(dashboardAgentConnection(input));
    52	  return `${setupPrompt(`Save this connection token with your file-writing tool to ${path}. Keep all characters unchanged. Set its directory to 0700 and the file to 0600. If damaged, use “Use a setup file” in CommonSwarm; do not repair credentials or paste them into chat.`)}\n\n${token}`;
    53	}
    54	
    55	/** Keep machine input out of Markdown prose, including embedded fence characters. */
    56	function codeBlock(language: string, text: string): string {
    57	  const fence = "`".repeat(Math.max(3, ...Array.from(text.matchAll(/`+/g), match => match[0].length + 1)));
    58	  return `${fence}${language}\n${text}\n${fence}`;
    59	}
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * What a copy out of the prompt block must place on the clipboard.
    63	 *
    64	 * A manual selection copy hands the target application BOTH `text/plain` and `text/html`. A
    65	 * Markdown-aware client converts that HTML rather than taking the plain text, and the conversion
    66	 * escapes every underscore as `\_` and rewrites a bare URL as a Markdown link — which is exactly
    67	 * the damage the code fences exist to prevent, arriving by a route the fences cannot reach
    68	 * (measured on a real hand-off, 2026-09-08). The block therefore writes plain text itself and
    69	 * suppresses the HTML flavour. A partial selection stays a partial selection; an empty one, which
    70	 * is what a keyboard copy with no range produces, falls back to the whole prompt.
    71	 */
    72	export function promptCopyPayload(selectionText: string, fullPrompt: string): string {
    73	  return selectionText.trim().length > 0 ? selectionText : fullPrompt;
    74	}

   103	  // before base32 decoding) would fail. To pass, an implementation must filter out all
   104	  // characters outside the base32 alphabet (A-Z, 2-7, and dot), ignoring backslashes and

   209	test("a lowercase token (must succeed)", () => {
   210	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   211	  // A wrong implementation that is strictly case-sensitive and only accepts uppercase
   212	  // RFC 4648 base32 letters (A-Z) would fail when input has been lowercased by a user
   213	  // or chat transformer. To pass, an implementation must normalize input with .toUpperCase()
   214	  // before matching the marker and decoding base32.
   215	  const env = connection();
   216	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   217

   219	  assert.notEqual(lower, token);
   220	  assert.deepEqual(decodeAgentConnectionToken(lower), env);
   221	  assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(lower), true);
   222	  assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(lower), env);
   223	
   224	  const mixed = token
   225	    .split("")
   226	    .map((c, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? c.toLowerCase() : c.toUpperCase()))
   227	    .join("");
   228	  assert.deepEqual(decodeAgentConnectionToken(mixed), env);
   229	  assert.equal(isAgentConnectionToken(mixed), true);
   230	  assert.deepEqual(parseAgentConnection(mixed), env);

   233	test("a truncated token (must fail on the checksum, not on JSON)", () => {
   234	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   235	  // A wrong implementation that attempts to decode and parse JSON before validating
   235	  // A wrong implementation that attempts to decode and parse JSON before validating
   236	  // the CRC32 checksum would fail with a JSON SyntaxError (e.g. "Unexpected end of JSON input")
   237	  // or a UTF-8 decoding error when given a truncated token, rather than detecting the corruption
   238	  // through the checksum. To pass, the implementation must verify the CRC32 checksum FIRST
   239	  // before attempting UTF-8 decoding or JSON parsing, throwing `token_checksum_invalid`.
   240	  const env = connection();
   241	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   242	
   243	  const originalJsonParse = JSON.parse;
   244	  let jsonParseCalls = 0;
   245	  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalJsonParse>) => {
   246	    jsonParseCalls++;
   247	    return originalJsonParse(...args);
   248	  };
   249	
   250	  try {
   251	    // 1. Cut 1 to 5 characters from the end of the token
   252	    for (let cut = 1; cut <= 5; cut++) {
   253	      const truncated = token.slice(0, -cut);
   254	      assert.throws(
   255	        () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(truncated),
   256	        (err: unknown) => {
   257	          assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   258	          assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   259	          assert.ok(!(err instanceof SyntaxError), "must not throw a JSON SyntaxError");
   260	          return true;
   261	        },
   262	      );
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    // 2. Cut characters from the body while keeping the checksum portion
   266	    const lastDot = token.lastIndexOf(".");
   267	    const prefixAndBody = token.slice(0, lastDot);
   268	    const checksumPart = token.slice(lastDot + 1);
   269	
   270	    for (let cut = 1; cut <= 5; cut++) {
   271	      const truncatedBody = `${prefixAndBody.slice(0, -cut)}.${checksumPart}`;
   272	      assert.throws(
   273	        () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(truncatedBody),
   274	        (err: unknown) => {
   275	          assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   276	          assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   277	          assert.ok(!(err instanceof SyntaxError), "must not throw a JSON SyntaxError");
   278	          return true;
   279	        },
   280	      );
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    // Prove checksum failure happened BEFORE JSON.parse or envelope validation was reached
   284	    assert.equal(jsonParseCalls, 0, "JSON.parse must never be reached for truncated tokens failing checksum");
   285	  } finally {
   286	    JSON.parse = originalJsonParse;
   287	  }
   288	});
