   290	test("one flipped character (must fail on the checksum)", () => {
   291	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   292	  // A wrong implementation that lacks CRC32 checksum verification or ignores checksum
   293	  // mismatches would decode corrupted payload data and fail only later during JSON parsing
   294	  // or semantic validation. To pass, an implementation must compute the CRC32 over the
   295	  // decoded body and reject any mismatch with `token_checksum_invalid`.
   296	  const env = connection();
   297	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   298	
   299	  const originalJsonParse = JSON.parse;
   300	  let jsonParseCalls = 0;
   301	  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalJsonParse>) => {
   302	    jsonParseCalls++;
   303	    return originalJsonParse(...args);
   304	  };
   305	
   306	  try {
   307	    // Flip a character in the token body
   308	    const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
   309	    const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
   310	    const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
   311	    const flippedBodyChar = token[bodyMid] === "A" ? "B" : "A";
   312	    const flippedBodyToken =
   313	      token.slice(0, bodyMid) + flippedBodyChar + token.slice(bodyMid + 1);
   314	
   315	    assert.throws(
   316	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedBodyToken),
   317	      (err: unknown) => {
   318	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   319	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   320	        return true;
   321	      },
   322	    );
   323	
   324	    // Flip a character in the checksum portion
   325	    const crcStart = bodyEnd + 1;
   326	    const crcIdx = crcStart + 2;
   327	    const flippedCrcChar = token[crcIdx] === "A" ? "B" : "A";
   328	    const flippedCrcToken =
   329	      token.slice(0, crcIdx) + flippedCrcChar + token.slice(crcIdx + 1);
   330	
   331	    assert.throws(
   332	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedCrcToken),
   333	      (err: unknown) => {
   334	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   335	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   336	        return true;
   337	      },
   338	    );
   339	
   340	    // Flip the final body symbol (must fail canonical base32 check, not reach JSON)
   341	    const finalBodyIdx = bodyEnd - 1;
   342	    const flippedFinalBodyChar = token[finalBodyIdx] === "U" ? "V" : "U";
   343	    const flippedFinalBodyToken =
   344	      token.slice(0, finalBodyIdx) + flippedFinalBodyChar + token.slice(bodyEnd);
   345	
   346	    assert.throws(
   347	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedFinalBodyToken),
   348	      (err: unknown) => {
   349	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   350	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   351	        return true;
   352	      },
   353	    );
   354	
   355	    // Flip the final checksum symbol (must fail canonical base32 check, not reach JSON)
   356	    const finalCrcIdx = token.length - 1;
   357	    const flippedFinalCrcChar = token[finalCrcIdx] === "Q" ? "R" : "Q";
   358	    const flippedFinalCrcToken =
   359	      token.slice(0, finalCrcIdx) + flippedFinalCrcChar;
   360	
   361	    assert.throws(
   362	      () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(flippedFinalCrcToken),
   363	      (err: unknown) => {
   364	        assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   365	        assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   366	        return true;
   367	      },
   368	    );
   369	
   370	    // Prove all checksum failures happened BEFORE JSON.parse or envelope validation was reached
   371	    assert.equal(jsonParseCalls, 0, "JSON.parse must never be reached for flipped characters failing checksum");
   372	  } finally {
   373	    JSON.parse = originalJsonParse;
   374	  }
   375	});

   377	test("an envelope whose credential is invalid (must fail in the existing validator, not the decoder)", () => {
   378	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   379	  // A wrong implementation that conflates token decoding with credential schema validation
   380	  // might throw a token decoder error (like `token_payload_invalid`) instead of delegating
   381	  // to the existing envelope and credential validators. Conversely, a wrong implementation
   382	  // that fails to execute the existing validator would accept malformed credentials.
   383	  // To pass, the token decoder must successfully decode the valid base32 envelope and pass
   384	  // it to `validateAgentConnectionEnvelope`, which preserves existing validator error codes.
   385	  const env = connection();
   386	
   387	  // Case 1: Principal mismatch between envelope principal_id and credential principal_id
   388	  const mismatchedEnvelope = {
   389	    ...env,
   390	    principal_id: OTHER,
   391	  };
   392	  const tokenMismatch = encodeAgentConnectionToken(mismatchedEnvelope);
   393	  assert.throws(
   394	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenMismatch),
   395	    (err: unknown) => {
   396	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   397	      assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "connection_identity_mismatch");
   398	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_payload_invalid");
   399	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_checksum_invalid");
   400	      return true;
   401	    },
   402	  );
   403	
   404	  // Case 2: Credential missing required agent_token
   405	  const invalidCredEnvelope = {
   406	    ...env,
   407	    credential: {
   408	      ...env.credential,
   409	      agent_token: undefined,
   410	    },
   411	  };
   412	  const tokenBadCred = encodeAgentConnectionToken(invalidCredEnvelope as unknown as AgentConnectionEnvelope);
   413	  assert.throws(
   414	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenBadCred),
   415	    (err: unknown) => {
   416	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentCredentialInputError, `expected AgentCredentialInputError but got ${err}`);
   417	      assert.equal(err.code, "agent_credential_missing_agent_token");
   418	      return true;
   419	    },
   420	  );
   421	
   422	  // Case 3: Credential is not an object
   423	  const nonObjectCredEnvelope = {
   424	    ...env,
   425	    credential: "not-an-object",
   426	  };
   427	  const tokenNonObj = encodeAgentConnectionToken(nonObjectCredEnvelope as unknown as AgentConnectionEnvelope);
   428	  assert.throws(
   429	    () => decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenNonObj),
   430	    (err: unknown) => {
   431	      assert.ok(err instanceof AgentSetupError, `expected AgentSetupError but got ${err}`);
   432	      assert.equal((err as AgentSetupError).code, "connection_invalid");
   433	      assert.notEqual((err as AgentSetupError).code, "token_payload_invalid");
   434	      return true;
   435	    },
   436	  );
   437	});

   439	test("a round trip: encode then decode returns the original bytes exactly", () => {
   440	  // Wrong implementation rationale:
   441	  // A wrong implementation that re-serializes JSON with altered key order, modifies
   442	  // whitespace or character encoding, or introduces padding corruption during base32
   443	  // encoding/decoding would fail to preserve the exact byte representation of the payload.
   444	  // To pass, the token encoder and decoder must preserve the exact UTF-8 byte stream
   445	  // without losing, reordering, or adding any bytes.
   445	  // without losing, reordering, or adding any bytes.
   446	  const env = connection();
   447	  const rawJson = JSON.stringify(env);
   448	  const originalBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(rawJson);
   449	
   450	  // Encode the envelope
   451	  const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
   452	
   453	  // Decode the token and assert envelope equality
   454	  const decoded = decodeAgentConnectionToken(token);
   455	  assert.deepEqual(decoded, env);
   456	
   457	  // Assert the UTF-8 JSON payload bytes match byte-for-byte
   458	  const parts = token.split(".");
   459	  assert.equal(parts.length, 3);
   460	  const payloadBytes = base32Decode(parts[1]!);
   461	  assert.deepEqual(payloadBytes, originalBytes);
   462	
   463	  // Assert TextDecoder recovers the exact original JSON string
   464	  const recoveredJson = new TextDecoder("utf-8").decode(payloadBytes);
   465	  assert.equal(recoveredJson, rawJson);
   466	
   467	  // Also verify encoding a direct string payload preserves exact bytes
   468	  const customJson = JSON.stringify(env, null, 2);
   469	  const customBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(customJson);
   470	  const tokenFromStr = encodeAgentConnectionToken(customJson);
   471	  const decodedFromStr = decodeAgentConnectionToken(tokenFromStr);
   472	  assert.deepEqual(decodedFromStr, env);
   473	  const partsFromStr = tokenFromStr.split(".");
   474	  const payloadBytesFromStr = base32Decode(partsFromStr[1]!);
   475	  assert.deepEqual(payloadBytesFromStr, customBytes);
   476	});

    73	  const requests: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    74	  const fetcher = (async (_input: unknown, init?: RequestInit) => {
    75	    const body = JSON.parse(String(init?.body));
    76	    requests.push(body);
    77	    assert.equal(new Headers(init?.headers).get("authorization"), `Bearer ${TOKEN}`);
    78	    assert.equal(init?.redirect, "error", "credentials must not follow a redirect");
    79	    let result;
     1	const UUID_RE =
     2	  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
     3	const AGENT_TOKEN_RE = /^swm_agt_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/;
     4	
     5	/* The credential artifact's `message` is validated byte-for-byte below, which makes it a
     6	 * PROTOCOL CONSTANT rather than copy: a site minting a different spelling is unreadable by
     7	 * every cswarm already installed. Measured against the shipped v0.1.15 binary, 2026-08-12.
     8	 *
     9	 * D-088. The current wording claims the credential is "bound to this task … so the agent's
    10	 * work stays scoped". The run binding and attribution are real; the task scope is not. So the
    11	 * honest spelling is accepted here first, and the site keeps emitting the current one until
    12	 * enough installs carry this build. Reversing that order trades a soft false claim for a hard
    13	 * break. */
    14	export const AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE =
    15	  "Agent credential minted. It is bound to this task and run so the agent's work stays scoped and attributable.";
    16	export const AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 =
    17	  "Agent credential minted. It is bound to this run, so the agent's work is attributable to it.";
    18	export const ACCEPTED_AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGES: readonly string[] = [
    19	  AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE,
    20	  AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
    21	];
    22	
    23	export interface AgentCredentialInput {
    24	  token: string;
    25	  principalId: string | null;
    26	  tokenId: string | null;
    27	  runId: string | null;
    28	  /** Epoch ms, when the artifact stated one. Null for a bare or pre-renewal artifact. */
    29	  expiresAt: number | null;
    30	  durable: boolean;
    31	}
    32	
    33	export type AgentCredentialInputErrorCode =
    34	  | "agent_credential_invalid_json"
    35	  | "agent_credential_not_object"
    36	  | "agent_credential_missing_agent_token"
    37	  | "agent_credential_invalid_agent_token"
    38	  | "agent_credential_fields_invalid";
    39	
    40	export type AgentCredentialInputSource =
    41	  | { kind: "file"; path: string }
    42	  | { kind: "stdin" };
    43	
    44	/** A stable structural reason for rejecting an agent credential input. */
    45	export class AgentCredentialInputError extends Error {
    46	  readonly name = "AgentCredentialInputError";
    47	
    48	  constructor(readonly code: AgentCredentialInputErrorCode, message: string) {
    49	    super(`[${code}] ${message}`);
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	
    53	export const AGENT_CREDENTIAL_REQUIRED_FIELDS = [
    54	  "message",
    55	  "status",
    56	  "principal_id",
    57	  "token_id",
    58	  "run_id",
    59	  "agent_token",
    60	] as const;
    61	export const AGENT_CREDENTIAL_OPTIONAL_FIELDS = ["expires_at"] as const;
    62	const ALLOWED_ARTIFACT_KEYS = new Set<string>([
    63	  ...AGENT_CREDENTIAL_REQUIRED_FIELDS,
    64	  ...AGENT_CREDENTIAL_OPTIONAL_FIELDS,
    65	]);
    66	
    67	function sourceName(source: AgentCredentialInputSource): string {
    68	  return source.kind === "file"
    69	    ? `agent credential file ${source.path}`
    70	    : "agent credential input from stdin";
    71	}
    72	
    73	function nextStep(source: AgentCredentialInputSource): string {
    74	  return source.kind === "file"
    75	    ? `Next step: copy the minted JSON line again and replace ${source.path}.`
    76	    : "Next step: copy the minted JSON line again and send that line to stdin.";
    77	}
    78	
    79	function credentialInputError(
    80	  code: AgentCredentialInputErrorCode,
    81	  source: AgentCredentialInputSource,
    82	  found: string,
    83	): AgentCredentialInputError {
    84	  return new AgentCredentialInputError(
    85	    code,
    86	    `${sourceName(source)} ${found}. It must be the JSON line CommonSwarm minted, copied unchanged: ${AGENT_CREDENTIAL_REQUIRED_FIELDS.join(", ")} (${AGENT_CREDENTIAL_OPTIONAL_FIELDS.join(", ")} is optional). ${nextStep(source)}`,
    87	  );
    88	}
    89	
    90	function jsonKind(value: unknown): string {
    91	  if (value === null) return "JSON null";
    92	  if (Array.isArray(value)) return "a JSON array";
    93	  return `a JSON ${typeof value}`;
    94	}
    95	
    96	/**
    97	 * Parses a minted JSON artifact or a legacy bare token. Faults are classified from parsed
    98	 * structure, never from another error's display text. No input value is copied into an error.
    99	 */
   100	export function parseAgentCredentialInput(
   101	  value: string,
   102	  source: AgentCredentialInputSource,
   103	): AgentCredentialInput {
   104	  // Bare credentials predate the artifact and remain valid for commands that do not need
   105	  // artifact metadata. Test the complete grammar so arbitrary non-JSON text cannot take this
   106	  // compatibility path.
   107	  if (AGENT_TOKEN_RE.test(value)) {
   108	    return {
   109	      token: value,
   110	      principalId: null,
   111	      tokenId: null,
   112	      runId: null,
   113	      expiresAt: null,
   114	      durable: false,
   115	    };
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  let parsed: unknown;
   119	  try {
   120	    parsed = JSON.parse(value) as unknown;
   121	  } catch {
   122	    throw credentialInputError(
   123	      "agent_credential_invalid_json",
   124	      source,
   125	      "contains text that is not valid JSON",
   126	    );
   127	  }
   128	  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
   129	    throw credentialInputError(
   130	      "agent_credential_not_object",
   131	      source,
   132	      `contains ${jsonKind(parsed)} instead of a JSON object`,
   133	    );
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  const artifact = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
   137	  if (!Object.hasOwn(artifact, "agent_token")) {
   138	    throw credentialInputError(
   139	      "agent_credential_missing_agent_token",
   140	      source,
   141	      'is missing "agent_token"',
   142	    );
   143	  }
   144	  if (typeof artifact.agent_token !== "string" ||
   145	      !AGENT_TOKEN_RE.test(artifact.agent_token)) {
   146	    const found = typeof artifact.agent_token === "string"
   147	      ? 'has "agent_token" as a string that is not a swm_agt_ credential'
   148	      : `has "agent_token" as ${jsonKind(artifact.agent_token)}`;
   149	    throw credentialInputError(
   150	      "agent_credential_invalid_agent_token",
   151	      source,
   152	      found,
   153	    );
   154	  }
   155	
   156	  const actualKeys = Object.keys(artifact);
   157	  const missingKeys = AGENT_CREDENTIAL_REQUIRED_FIELDS.filter((key) =>
   158	    !Object.hasOwn(artifact, key)
   159	  );
   160	  const unknownCount = actualKeys.filter((key) =>
   161	    !ALLOWED_ARTIFACT_KEYS.has(key)
   162	  ).length;
   163	  const invalidKeys: string[] = [];
   164	  if (Object.hasOwn(artifact, "message") &&
   165	      !ACCEPTED_AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGES.includes(artifact.message as string)) {
   166	    invalidKeys.push("message");
   167	  }
   168	  if (Object.hasOwn(artifact, "status") && artifact.status !== "accepted") {
   169	    invalidKeys.push("status");
   170	  }
   171	  for (const key of ["principal_id", "token_id", "run_id"] as const) {
   172	    if (Object.hasOwn(artifact, key) &&
   173	        (typeof artifact[key] !== "string" || !UUID_RE.test(artifact[key]))) {
   174	      invalidKeys.push(key);
   175	    }
   176	  }
   177	  if (artifact.expires_at !== undefined) {
   178	    if (typeof artifact.expires_at !== "string" ||
   179	        Number.isNaN(Date.parse(artifact.expires_at))) {
   180	      invalidKeys.push("expires_at");
   181	    }
   182	  }
   183	  if (missingKeys.length > 0 || unknownCount > 0 || invalidKeys.length > 0) {
   184	    const faults: string[] = [];
   185	    if (unknownCount > 0) {
   186	      faults.push(`has ${unknownCount} unrecognized ${unknownCount === 1 ? "field" : "fields"}`);
   187	    }
   188	    if (missingKeys.length > 0) {
   189	      faults.push(`is missing required ${missingKeys.length === 1 ? "field" : "fields"} ${missingKeys.map((key) => `"${key}"`).join(", ")}`);
   190	    }
   191	    if (invalidKeys.length > 0) {
   192	      faults.push(`has invalid ${invalidKeys.length === 1 ? "field" : "fields"} ${invalidKeys.map((key) => `"${key}"`).join(", ")}`);
   193	    }
   194	    throw credentialInputError(
   195	      "agent_credential_fields_invalid",
   196	      source,
   197	      faults.join(" and "),
   198	    );
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  const expiresAt = artifact.expires_at === undefined
   202	    ? null
   203	    : Date.parse(artifact.expires_at as string);
   204	  return {
   205	    token: artifact.agent_token,
   206	    principalId: (artifact.principal_id as string).toLowerCase(),
   207	    tokenId: (artifact.token_id as string).toLowerCase(),
   208	    runId: (artifact.run_id as string).toLowerCase(),
   209	    expiresAt,
   210	    durable: true,
   211	  };
   212	}

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}

+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}

+  }));

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}

codex
The new browser codec is isolated: the site build passes, its emitted browser chunk has no Node builtin or browser-external shim, and an independent bundle graph excludes `agent-connection-token.ts`. One claimed fix is still suspect: detection and decoding do not call the same normalizer. I have reproduced disagreements and am checking whether they affect the routing boundary.

+    fn();
+    return "ok";

+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "okok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));

+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

     6	
     7	/*
     8	 * browser bundle guard — no root-only release metadata may leak into emitted browser JS.
     9	 *
    10	 * WHY IT EXISTS. When `lib/install.ts` imported `lib/release.ts`, the AgentConnect browser
    11	 * bundle pulled in the repo-root package.json and src/cloud/config.ts (which imports
    12	 * node:crypto). A clean build warned that node:crypto was externalized for browser
    13	 * compatibility, and the emitted JS contained root-only strings that only belong in the
    14	 * release toolchain — the npm script names `build:command-core` and `test:p1-server`,
    15	 * plus `node:crypto` itself, the leaked import that named the warning. `install.ts` is
    16	 * browser-safe again (release.ts imports install.ts, never the reverse), and this scan
    17	 * pins the absence into the gate.
    18	 *
    19	 * POSITIVE CONTROL. Absence assertions pass vacuously if the glob is wrong or the scan is
    20	 * empty, so the same scan must also find an ordinary, known browser-bundle string in at
    21	 * least one emitted file. `navigator.clipboard` is emitted by CodeBlock.astro's copy
    22	 * script on /download; finding it proves the scanner actually reads the emitted JS.
    23	 *
    24	 * Picked up by `npm --prefix site test` through the `scripts/*.test.mjs` glob. Requires a
    25	 * prior site build — it reads `site/dist/_astro`.
    26	 */
    27	
    28	const SENTINELS = ["build:command-core", "test:p1-server", "node:crypto"];
    29	const KNOWN_BROWSER_STRING = "navigator.clipboard";
    30	
    31	const siteDir = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
    32	const astroDir = join(siteDir, "dist", "_astro");
    33	
    34	/** Every emitted JS file under dist/_astro continued is read here. */
    35	function emittedBrowserJsFiles() {
    36	  const files = [];
    37	  const walk = (dir) => {
    38	    for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    39	      const path = join(dir, entry);
    40	      if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) {
    41	        walk(path);
    42	      } else if (path.endsWith(".js")) {
    43	        files.push(path);
    44	      }
    45	    }
    46	  };
    47	  walk(astroDir);
    48	  return files;
    49	}
    50	
    51	test("browser bundle guard: no emitted browser JS contains root-only release metadata", () => {
    52	  const files = emittedBrowserJsFiles();
    53	  assert.ok(
    54	    files.length > 0,
    55	    `emitted browser JS must exist under ${astroDir} for the scan to mean anything`,
    56	  );
    57	
    58	  let knownStringFound = false;
    59	  for (const file of files) {
    60	    const source = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
    61	    for (const sentinel of SENTINELS) {
    62	      assert.equal(
    63	        source.includes(sentinel),
    64	        false,
    65	        `${sentinel} leaked into the browser bundle ${file}`,
    66	      );
    67	    }
    68	    if (source.includes(KNOWN_BROWSER_STRING)) knownStringFound = true;
    69	  }
    70

    72	    knownStringFound,
    73	    true,
    74	    `positive control: the scanner must find an ordinary browser-bundle string ` +
    75	      `(${KNOWN_BROWSER_STRING}) across ${files.length} emitted file(s), or the absence ` +
    76	      `assertions above would be vacuous`,

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

exec

# Independent D-036 review arm

Reviewed SHA `99fc790d758a788016883063c718e662f91063d6` against `origin/main` at `ba998707b272355c893995fefb9dc75534afce3a` (v0.1.66). The scope matches the token-handoff purpose.

First diff line: `diff --git a/site/src/components/connect/agent-prompt.observer.test.ts b/site/src/components/connect/agent-prompt.observer.test.ts`

## Blocking findings

1. **[P1, confidence 10/10] A one-character-flipped token can pass the checksum and reach JSON and credential validation.** `base32Decode` at `src/cloud/agent-connection-token.ts:38-52` drops unused final bits. The decoder at lines 175-190 does not require the one allowed base32 spelling and accepts any checksum with at least four decoded bytes. On this SHA, changing the final body symbol `U` to `V` kept the decoded body byte-for-byte equal and was accepted. Changing the final checksum symbol `Q` to `R` also kept the decoded checksum equal and was accepted. Appending `A` to the checksum and appending `.EXTRA` were accepted too. This refutes the required single-character-flip and strict-structure claims. Fix: extract the token boundary, require the exact checksum length, require exactly four checksum bytes, and re-encode each decoded part to prove its base32 spelling is canonical (the one allowed spelling). Add final-symbol and extra-part controls.

2. **[P1, confidence 10/10] Recoverable formatting inside the marker is rejected by the public setup path.** `decodeAgentConnectionToken` cleans formatting before it finds `CSWARMA.`, but `isAgentConnectionToken` at `src/cloud/agent-connection-token.ts:70-75` searches the uncleaned text. `CSWA**RMA.<body>.<crc>`, `CSWA\nRMA...`, and `CSWA\tRMA...` all decoded when called directly, but `parseAgentConnection` classified each as JSON and returned `connection_invalid`. A bold marker in the body works; the same marker in the prefix does not. Fix: after the leading-JSON guard, use the same safe normalization for detection and decoding. Test formatting at every marker boundary.

3. **[P2, confidence 10/10] Two controls do not prove their stated claim.** At `tests/p1-cli/agent-connection-token.test.ts:356`, the credential test accepts either `agent_credential_missing_agent_token` or generic `connection_invalid` and falls back to `err.message`. A wrong early rejection can pass, and the fallback classifies by presentation text contrary to D-053. At lines 230-314, checksum tests assert only the final code; code could call `JSON.parse` first, catch it, and then report `token_checksum_invalid`. Fix: assert the exact typed credential error, and add observable zero-call seams for UTF-8, JSON, and envelope validation on checksum failures.

4. **[P2, confidence 9/10] The browser entry imports a Node-only dependency graph.** `site/src/components/connect/agent-prompt.ts:7` imports the combined encoder/decoder module. That module imports `config.ts`, which imports `node:crypto`. Astro built the site but warned that `node:crypto` was externalized. A direct browser `esbuild` failed to resolve it. Astro removed the Node code from the emitted AgentConnect chunk, so I did not find a current browser runtime failure. Fix: put the encoder, base32, and CRC code in a browser-safe codec module; keep target validation and decoding in the Node-side module.

## Required checks

1. **Alphabet, separator, and prefix:** The prefix is `CSWARMA`, made only from A-Z. The body and checksum encoder use only `ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567`. The only punctuation is `.`. Generated tokens contained no underscore, backslash, bracket, colon, slash, space, or URL scheme. The exact unknown Markdown converter was not available, so I could not prove that no converter will linkify a dotted string. The existing link-wrapper cases decode when the marker stays intact.

2. **Fail-closed order:** The source order is marker, structure, base32 decode, checksum, UTF-8, JSON, then envelope and credential validation. Truncations covered by the suite fail with `token_checksum_invalid` before JSON. The order is correct for those inputs. It is not fail-closed for non-canonical base32 or extra structure, as finding 1 proves.

3. **Recoverable damage:** Lowercase, body line wrapping, inserted body spaces/tabs, broken fences, labeled fences, ordinary prose, link wrappers, and a bold marker inside the body decode. Formatting inside `CSWARMA.` fails public detection. Prose that starts with `{` also fails because the JSON preference is unconditional, even though direct token decoding succeeds.

4. **JSON versus token:** The earlier JSON bug is fixed for valid envelopes. I constructed a valid envelope whose `anon_key` contains the exact text `CSWARMA.`; `isAgentConnectionToken` returned false and the JSON path returned the expected principal. The current rule can still misclassify brace-led non-JSON prose containing a token, as noted above.

5. **Credential handling:** In the changed production code, the credential payload is not put in argv, environment variables, logs, or URLs. Setup argv carries only the private file path. Token error messages use fixed text and do not echo the token or decoded payload. Existing end-to-end tests prove setup output omits the credential. The new token-negative tests do not exercise CLI output or assert secret absence, so a future token-error leak could pass them.

6. **Old JSON setup path:** It remains active and passed the full P1 CLI gate. `setupAgent` still returns `profile: profilePath`, writes the profile after identity checks, and the existing CLI fixture uses the saved profile successfully. The new token test does not assert its returned `profile` field, so that specific control could pass if the field were removed.

7. **AGENTS.md controls:** User-facing connection-field lists come from `AGENT_CONNECTION_FIELDS`; encode and decode use one base32 alphabet; receive lists still come from their enforced constants. I found no production branch on `error.message`. The test at line 356 does use `err.message` as a fallback classifier and must be made typed. The JSON and token paths also duplicate envelope and target validation instead of calling one shared validator; they match now but can drift.

8. **Test discrimination:**

| Hostile case | What the current test proves | How a wrong implementation can still pass |
|---|---|---|
| Safe alphabet and prefix | One generated fixture matches `CSWARMA.[A-Z2-7]+.[A-Z2-7]+`. | Emit safe text for this fixture but unsafe text for another byte pattern; there is no exhaustive or property control. Source inspection closes this current-code gap. |
| Truncation and middle-symbol flips | Selected cuts and middle changes report the checksum code. | Ignore final pad bits or extra checksum bytes, as this SHA does; or parse first and translate the later error to the expected code. |
| Spaces, tabs, and line wrapping | Noise after the intact marker is removed. | Require `CSWARMA.` to stay contiguous. Every current wrap starts after the marker. |
| Bold Markdown marker | No explicit bold case exists. Body `**` works only because all non-alphabet text is deleted. | Reject bold, spaces, or a line break inside the marker and still pass every current test. |
| Fences, labels, links, and prose | An intact marker can be found inside the listed wrappers. | Accept the first three dot parts and ignore extra parts or trailing decoded bytes. That permissive parser passes these tests and also accepts malformed structure. |
| Lowercase | Whole-token lower and mixed case are normalized. | Normalize case while keeping the marker-contiguity and canonical-base32 defects. |
| JSON discrimination | Valid JSON beginning with `{` wins even when values contain `cswarm` or `cswarma`. | Special-case every leading `{` as JSON. That passes the cases but rejects a token buried in brace-led prose. |
| Credential validation | Some invalid envelopes are rejected. | Return generic `connection_invalid`, or match presentation text, and still satisfy the loose assertion at line 356. |
| Secret-free failure | Damaged plain JSON is checked through CLI output. | Echo an encoded token on a token-specific error and still pass all new token-negative tests. |
| Legacy profile result | JSON and token setup connect through fake HTTP fixtures. | Omit or change the returned `profile` field; the new control does not assert it. |

================================================================
1. Encoded token charset (alphabet, separator, prefix)
================================================================

src/cloud/agent-connection-token.ts:
- BASE32_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567" (RFC 4648, no padding `=`)
- TOKEN_VERSION = "A"
- TOKEN_PREFIX = `CSWARM${TOKEN_VERSION}` → "CSWARMA"
- TOKEN_MARKER = "CSWARMA."
- encode: `${TOKEN_MARKER}${base32Encode(jsonBytes)}.${base32Encode(crc32Bytes)}`

A live dashboard-shaped token on this SHA was 946 chars. Unique charset was exactly `.234567ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ`. No `_`, `-`, `+`, `/`, `:`, backtick, `[]()`, or `://`. Prefix "CSWARMA" is only alphabet characters (A-Z2-7). The earlier digit-outside-2-7 footgun is not present: cleaner `replace(/[^A-Z2-7.]/g, "")` would eat a `1` in `CSWARM1.`; it does not eat `A`.

The only punctuation is `.` (two structural separators). CommonMark can backslash-escape `.`. A converter that emits `\.` is recovered because `\` is stripped. GFM autolink wants `http(s):`, `www.`, or email, not this form. Body prefix for the dashboard object order (`{"version":1…`) encodes as `PMRHMZLS…`, not a common TLD. Markdown link wrapping is recovered (claim 3).

I could not put a Markdown-emphasis or URL-scheme character into an encoded token without changing the encoder.

decodeAgentConnectionToken (agent-connection-token.ts:153-211):

+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,

+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,

+import {
+  decodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  encodeAgentConnectionToken,
+  isAgentConnectionToken,
+} from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-connection-token.js";
+import { parseAgentConnection } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-profile.js";
+import { AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088 } from "../../review-77fe6df4/src/cloud/agent-credential-input.js";
+
+const id = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
+const env = {
+  version: 1 as const,
+  url: "https://fixture.example",
+  anon_key: "public-fixture",
+  workspace_id: "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
+  principal_id: id,
+  credential: {
+    message: AGENT_CREDENTIAL_MESSAGE_D088,
+    status: "accepted",
+    principal_id: id,
+    token_id: "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
+    run_id: "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222",
+    agent_token: `swm_agt_${"A".repeat(43)}`,
+    expires_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
+  },
+};
+
+const token = encodeAgentConnectionToken(env);
+const bodyStart = token.indexOf(".") + 1;
+const bodyEnd = token.lastIndexOf(".");
+const bodyMid = Math.floor((bodyStart + bodyEnd) / 2);
+
+const variants: Record<string, string> = {
+  canonical: token,
+  marker_hyphen: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA-RMA"),
+  marker_bang: token.replace("CSWARMA", "CSWA!RMA"),
+  leading_brace: `{ ${token}`,
+  json_string_value: JSON.stringify({ token }),
+  extra_part_direct: `${token}.EXTRA`,
+  extra_part_after_tab: `${token}\t.EXTRA`,
+  extra_checksum_direct: `${token}A`,
+  extra_checksum_after_tab: `${token}\tA`,
+  punctuation_in_checksum: `${token.slice(0, -3)}!${token.slice(-3)}`,
+  bold_in_body: `${token.slice(0, bodyMid)}**${token.slice(bodyMid)}`,
+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}

+  tabs_wrapped: `\t${token.match(/.{1,31}/g)!.join("\t\n")}\t`,
+  lowercase: token.toLowerCase(),
+  fenced: `\`\`\`text\n${token}\n\`\`\``,
+  prose: `Use this token: ${token} to connect.`,
+};
+
+function result(fn: () => unknown): string {
+  try {
+    fn();
+    return "ok";
+  } catch (error) {
+    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
+    return `${e.constructor.name}:${e.code ?? e.name}`;
+  }
+}
+
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(variants)) {
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({
+    name,
+    detected: isAgentConnectionToken(raw),
+    decode: result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw)),
+    parse: result(() => parseAgentConnection(raw)),
+  }));
+}
+
+// Exhaust the non-significant final-symbol bits for both Base32 fields. Only the
+// canonical final symbol may decode for a fixed body/checksum.
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
+for (const [partName, dotIndex] of [["body", bodyEnd], ["checksum", token.length]] as const) {
+  const index = dotIndex - 1;
+  const accepted: string[] = [];
+  for (const c of alphabet) {
+    const mutated = token.slice(0, index) + c + token.slice(index + 1);
+    if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") accepted.push(c);
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ partName, original: token[index], accepted }));
+}
+
+let mutationCount = 0;
+let mutationAccepted = 0;
+for (let n = 0; n < 12; n++) {
+  const sampleToken = encodeAgentConnectionToken({ ...env, anon_key: `${env.anon_key}${"x".repeat(n)}` });
+  const sampleBodyEnd = sampleToken.lastIndexOf(".");
+  for (const index of [sampleBodyEnd - 1, sampleToken.length - 1]) {
+    for (const c of alphabet) {
+      if (c === sampleToken[index]) continue;
+      mutationCount++;
+      const mutated = sampleToken.slice(0, index) + c + sampleToken.slice(index + 1);
+      if (result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(mutated)) === "ok") mutationAccepted++;
+    }
+  }
+}
+console.log(JSON.stringify({ mutationCount, mutationAccepted }));
+
+const originalParse = JSON.parse;
+for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries({
+  extra_part_direct: variants.extra_part_direct,
+  extra_checksum_direct: variants.extra_checksum_direct,
+  extra_part_after_tab: variants.extra_part_after_tab,
+})) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  JSON.parse = (...args: Parameters<typeof originalParse>) => {
+    calls++;
+    return originalParse(...args);
+  };
+  const outcome = result(() => decodeAgentConnectionToken(raw));
+  JSON.parse = originalParse;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, outcome, jsonParseCalls: calls }));
+}
